result11. Command output
    for row in plan_rows:
        if row["item_id"] in item_ids:
            add_issue("schema", 1.0, f"duplicate item_id {row['item_id']}")
        item_ids.add(row["item_id"])

        row_type = row["row_type"]
        if row_type not in {"trip", "charge"}:
            add_issue("schema", 1.0, f"invalid row_type {row_type}")
            continue

        if row_type == "trip":
            trip_id = row["trip_id"]
            trip_rows_seen.add(trip_id)
            if trip_id not in trip_lookup:
                add_issue("coverage", 1.0, f"unknown trip_id {trip_id}")
                continue
            trip = trip_lookup[trip_id]
            decision = row["decision"]
            if decision not in {"operate", "cancel"}:
                add_issue("schema", 1.0, f"invalid trip decision for {trip_id}")
            if row["charger_id"] or row["start_min"] or row["end_min"] or row["energy_kwh"]:
                add_issue("nullability", 1.0, f"trip row must leave charge fields blank for {trip_id}")
            if row["item_id"] != trip_id:
                add_issue("schema", 1.0, f"trip item_id must equal trip_id for {trip_id}")
            if decision == "cancel":
                if trip["priority"] == "critical":
                    add_issue("service", 1.0, f"critical trip canceled: {trip_id}")
                if row["bus_id"]:
                    add_issue("nullability", 1.0, f"canceled trip must leave bus blank: {trip_id}")
            else:
                bus_id = row["bus_id"]
                if bus_id not in buses:
                    add_issue("assignment", 1.0, f"unknown bus {bus_id} on {trip_id}")
                    continue
                bus = buses[bus_id]
                if not compatible(bus, trip):
                    add_issue("compatibility", 1.0, f"incompatible bus {bus_id} on {trip_id}")
                timelines[bus_id].append(
                    {
                        "type": "trip",
                        "trip_id": trip_id,
                        "start": float(to_int(trip["depart_min"])),
                        "end": float(to_int(trip["arrive_min"])),
                        "start_loc": trip["start_location"],
                        "end_loc": trip["end_location"],
                        "energy": effective_trip_energy(bus_id, trip, derates),
                        "min_start_soc": to_float(trip["min_start_soc_kwh"]),
                    }
                )
        else:
            if row["trip_id"]:
                add_issue("nullability", 1.0, f"charge row must leave trip blank: {row['item_id']}")
            if row["decision"] != "charge":
                add_issue("schema", 1.0, f"charge row must use decision=charge: {row['item_id']}")
            bus_id = row["bus_id"]
            charger_id = row["charger_id"]
            if bus_id not in buses:
                add_issue("assignment", 1.0, f"unknown charge bus {bus_id}")
                continue
            if charger_id not in chargers:
                add_issue("assignment", 1.0, f"unknown charger {charger_id}")
                continue
            if not row["start_min"] or not row["end_min"] or not row["energy_kwh"]:
                add_issue("nullability", 1.0, f"charge row missing numeric fields: {row['item_id']}")
                continue
            start = to_float(row["start_min"])
            end = to_float(row["end_min"])
            energy = to_float(row["energy_kwh"])
            if end <= start + EPS:
                add_issue("charge", 1.0, f"nonpositive charge duration: {row['item_id']}")
            if energy < -EPS:
                add_issue("charge", 1.0, f"negative charge energy: {row['item_id']}")
            bus = buses[bus_id]
            charger = chargers[charger_id]
            if (bus_id, charger_id) not in compat:
                add_issue("compatibility", 1.0, f"incompatible charger {charger_id} for bus {bus_id}")
            cap = charge_capacity_kwh(bus, charger, start, end, data["derates"])
            if energy > cap + EPS:
                add_issue("charge", (energy - cap) / max(1.0, cap), f"charge energy exceeds capacity: {row['item_id']}")
            session = {
                "type": "charge",
                "item_id": row["item_id"],
                "start": start,
                "end": end,
                "start_loc": "DEPOT",
                "end_loc": "DEPOT",
                "energy": energy,
                "charger_id": charger_id,
            }
            timelines[bus_id].append(session)
            charger_sessions[charger_id].append(session)
            charge_rows.append({"bus_id": bus_id, "charger_id": charger_id, "start": start, "end": end, "energy": energy})

    visible_trip_ids = {trip["trip_id"] for trip in data["trips"]}
    missing = visible_trip_ids - trip_rows_seen
    extra = trip_rows_seen - visible_trip_ids
    if missing:
        add_issue("coverage", len(missing) / max(1, len(visible_trip_ids)), f"missing trip rows: {sorted(missing)}")
    if extra:
        add_issue("coverage", len(extra) / max(1, len(visible_trip_ids)), f"extra trip rows: {sorted(extra)}")

    for charger_id, sessions in charger_sessions.items():
        charger = chargers[charger_id]
        setup = to_float(charger["setup_min"])
        sessions.sort(key=lambda event: (event["start"], event["end"], event.get("item_id", "")))
        for prev, nxt in zip(sessions, sessions[1:]):
            gap = nxt["start"] - prev["end"]
            if gap + EPS < setup:
                add_issue(
                    "charger_conflict",
                    (setup - gap) / max(1.0, setup),
                    f"charger {charger_id} lacks setup gap between {prev.get('item_id', '?')} and {nxt.get('item_id', '?')}",
                )

    for outage in data["outages"]:
        charger_id = outage["charger_id"]
        start = to_int(outage["start_min"])
        end = to_int(outage["end_min"])
        for session in charger_sessions.get(charger_id, []):
            if overlap(session["start"], session["end"], start, end) > 0:
                add_issue("outage", 1.0, f"charge {session.get('item_id', '?')} overlaps outage on {charger_id}")

    for bus_id, events in timelines.items():
        bus = buses[bus_id]
        events.sort(key=lambda event: (event["start"], 0 if event["type"] == "trip" else 1, event["end"]))
        current_loc = "DEPOT"
        current_time = 0.0
        soc = to_float(bus["initial_soc_kwh"])
        for event in events:
            event["initial_soc"] = to_float(bus["initial_soc_kwh"])
            travel = data["deadhead"].get((current_loc, event["start_loc"]))
            if travel is None:
                add_issue("timing", 1.0, f"missing deadhead from {current_loc} to {event['start_loc']}")
                travel = 10**9
            if current_time + travel > event["start"] + EPS:
                add_issue(
                    "timing",
                    (current_time + travel - event["start"]) / max(1.0, event["start"] - current_time),
                    f"bus {bus_id} cannot reach {event['type']} starting at {event['start']}",
                )
            if event["type"] == "trip":
                if soc + EPS < event["min_start_soc"]:
                    add_issue(
                        "soc",
                        (event["min_start_soc"] - soc) / max(1.0, event["min_start_soc"]),
                        f"bus {bus_id} below trip start reserve on {event['trip_id']}",
                    )
                event["soc_before"] = soc
                soc -= event["energy"]
                event["soc_after"] = soc
                if soc + EPS < to_float(bus["min_soc_kwh"]):
                    add_issue(
                        "soc",
                        (to_float(bus["min_soc_kwh"]) - soc) / max(1.0, to_float(bus["min_soc_kwh"])),
                        f"bus {bus_id} below minimum SOC after {event['trip_id']}",
                    )
            else:
                event["soc_before"] = soc
                soc += event["energy"]
                event["soc_after"] = soc
                if soc > to_float(bus["max_soc_kwh"]) + EPS:

def build_baseline_plan(data: Dict[str, object]) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
    rows: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
    for trip in data["trips"]:
        rows.append(
            {
                "row_type": "trip",
                "item_id": trip["trip_id"],
                "bus_id": trip["block_hint"],
                "trip_id": trip["trip_id"],
                "charger_id": "",
                "start_min": "",
                "end_min": "",
                "energy_kwh": "",
                "decision": "operate",
            }
        )
    return rows


def write_plan(path: Path, rows: List[Dict[str, str]]) -> None:
    with path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=PLAN_HEADER)
        writer.writeheader()
        for row in rows:
            writer.writerow({key: row.get(key, "") for key in PLAN_HEADER})


def parse_plan(path: Path) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
    with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        reader = csv.DictReader(handle)
        if reader.fieldnames != PLAN_HEADER:
            raise ValueError(f"CSV header mismatch: {reader.fieldnames}")
        return list(reader)


def effective_trip_energy(bus_id: str, trip: Dict[str, str], derates: List[Dict[str, str]]) -> float:
    energy = to_float(trip["energy_kwh"])
    depart = to_int(trip["depart_min"])
    arrive = to_int(trip["arrive_min"])
    multiplier = 1.0
    for row in derates:
        if row["bus_id"] != bus_id:
            continue
        start = to_int(row["start_min"])
        end = to_int(row["end_min"])
        if overlap(depart, arrive, start, end) > 0:
            multiplier = max(multiplier, to_float(row["energy_multiplier"]))
    return energy * multiplier


def charge_capacity_kwh(
    bus: Dict[str, str],
    charger: Dict[str, str],
    start: float,
    end: float,
    derates: List[Dict[str, str]],
) -> float:
    if end <= start + EPS:
        return 0.0
    points = {start, end}
    for row in derates:
        if row["bus_id"] != bus["bus_id"]:
            continue
        ds = to_int(row["start_min"])
        de = to_int(row["end_min"])
        if overlap(start, end, ds, de) > 0:
            points.add(float(ds))
            points.add(float(de))
    ordered = sorted(points)
    total = 0.0
    for left, right in zip(ordered, ordered[1:]):
        if right <= left + EPS:
            continue
        rate = min(to_float(bus["max_charge_kw"]), to_float(charger["power_kw"]))
        for row in derates:
            if row["bus_id"] != bus["bus_id"]:
                continue
            ds = to_int(row["start_min"])
            de = to_int(row["end_min"])
            if overlap(left, right, ds, de) > 0:
                rate = min(rate, to_float(row["max_charge_kw"]))
        total += rate * (right - left) / 60.0
    return total


def locate_checkpoint_state(events: List[Dict[str, object]], checkpoint: int) -> Tuple[Optional[Dict[str, object]], Optional[Dict[str, object]], float]:
    prev_event = None
    next_event = None
    soc = None
    for event in events:
        if event["start"] <= checkpoint < event["end"]:
            return event, event, event["soc_before"]
        if event["end"] <= checkpoint + EPS:
            prev_event = event
            soc = event["soc_after"]
        if checkpoint <= event["start"] - EPS:
            next_event = event
            break
    if soc is None:
        soc = events[0]["initial_soc"] if events else 0.0
    return prev_event, next_event, soc


def build_bus_timelines(
    plan_rows: List[Dict[str, str]],
    data: Dict[str, object],
    issues: List[Dict[str, object]],
) -> Dict[str, List[Dict[str, object]]]:
    buses = data["buses"]
    chargers = data["chargers"]
    compat = data["compat"]
    deadhead = data["deadhead"]
    derates = data["derates"]
    trip_lookup = {trip["trip_id"]: trip for trip in data["trips"]}

    timelines: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, object]]] = defaultdict(list)
    charger_sessions: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, object]]] = defaultdict(list)
    charge_rows: List[Dict[str, object]] = []

    trip_rows_seen = set()
    item_ids = set()

    def add_issue(group: str, residual: float, detail: str) -> None:
        issues.append({"group": group, "residual": max(0.0, residual), "detail": detail})

    for row in plan_rows:

# Initial Model: Visible Trips at Minute 0

`/app/AGENT_BRIEF.md` is not present in the workspace, so this model uses the public rules in `/app/PROBLEM_STATEMENT.md`, `/app/EVENT_NOTICE.md`, and the visible files under `/app/data/`.

## Visible Decision Scope

- Time horizon: the currently visible full day, with `current_min = 0`.
- Trips: every row in `data/trips.csv` with `reveal_step <= visible_step`.
- Buses: all rows in `data/buses.csv`.
- Chargers: all rows in `data/chargers.csv`.
- Current packet facts:
  - no active charger outage,
  - no active bus derating,
  - no event-specific charger session restriction,
  - no frozen prior commitments.

## Stage Strategy

The solver uses a hybrid two-stage method.

1. Build a constructive incumbent from the published `block_hint` values.
2. Audit the exported artifact exactly against the public hard rules.
3. If the incumbent is infeasible, invoke a SCIP restricted repair MIP with a warm start.
4. If the incumbent is feasible and has no cancellations and no charging rows, stop in performance mode because every public objective term is nonnegative and the resulting objective is exactly `0`.

For this initial packet the constructive incumbent is already feasible:

- each visible trip is assigned to its hinted bus,
- no charging sessions are needed,
- all trip start-SOC and terminal SOC checks pass,
- deadhead timing between consecutive hinted trips passes,
- four unused buses remain at the depot all day, so each reserve checkpoint is satisfied.

Because the plan has no canceled trips and no charging rows, every public objective component is zero:

- cancellation penalties: `0`,
- electricity purchases: `0`,
- carbon cost: `0`,
- battery-stress cost: `0`,
- change penalties: `0` at the initial step.

That gives a certificate of optimality without expanding the charging MIP.

## Constructive Incumbent

For each visible trip `t`, let `hint(t)` be its `block_hint` bus. The incumbent sets:

- `operate(t) = 1`,
- `assign(hint(t), t) = 1`,
- no `charge` rows.

The audit then verifies:

- exact trip-row coverage,
- bus-class and low-floor compatibility,
- chronological feasibility from the depot to the first trip and between consecutive trips using `deadhead_times.csv`,
- battery state propagation:
  - initial SOC from `buses.csv`,
  - trip energy from `trips.csv`,
  - start reserve `min_start_soc_kwh`,
  - lower bound `min_soc_kwh`,
  - upper bound `max_soc_kwh`,
- reserve checkpoints from `reserve_requirements.csv`,
- CSV nullability rules from the problem statement.

## Restricted Repair MIP

The fallback MIP is only used if the constructive incumbent fails.

### Decision variables

- `x[b,t] in {0,1}`: bus `b` operates trip `t`.
- `z[t] in {0,1}`: trip `t` is canceled.

### Objective

Minimize

`sum_t cancel_penalty[t] * z[t]`

This is sufficient for restoration because all charging-related cost terms are nonnegative and the fallback MIP is entered only to repair hard-rule violations in the zero-charge incumbent.

### Constraints

- Trip coverage:
  - `sum_b x[b,t] + z[t] = 1` for every visible trip `t`.
- Critical service:
  - `z[t] = 0` for every critical trip.
- Bus-trip compatibility:
  - `x[b,t] = 0` if bus class or low-floor capability is insufficient.
  - `artic` buses may cover `standard` trips; `standard` buses may not cover `artic` trips.
- Depot reachability for the first assigned trip:
  - disallow `x[b,t] = 1` if `deadhead(DEPOT, start(t)) > depart(t)`.
- Pairwise time-feasibility:
  - for any pair with `depart(i) < depart(j)` and
    `arrive(i) + deadhead(end(i), start(j)) > depart(j)`,
    enforce `x[b,i] + x[b,j] <= 1`.
- Start-SOC reserve:
  - if bus `b` operates trip `t`, then
    `initial_soc[b] - sum_{k: depart(k) < depart(t)} energy[k] * x[b,k] >= min_start_soc[t]`.
- End-of-horizon minimum SOC:
  - `initial_soc[b] - sum_t energy[t] * x[b,t] >= min_soc[b]`.

The repair MIP is intentionally restricted. It is a restoration operator, not the primary optimizer for this packet.

## Independent Audit and `theta`

The script computes a hard-constraint residual summary directly from the exported CSV, not from solver variables. The main state vector includes:

- artifact presence and schema status,
- incumbent count,
- maximum normalized hard-constraint residual `theta`,
- number of violated hard-rule groups,
- integrality residual,
- decision-universe coverage,
- objective / best bound / relative gap when SCIP is used,
- elapsed time.

`theta = 0` is required before entering performance mode.

## Method

    for bus_id,bus in buses.items():
        seq=sorted(by_bus.get(bus_id,[]), key=lambda x:int(x['depart_min']))
        if not seq:
            if float(bus['initial_soc_kwh']) + 1e-8 >= thr:
                count += 1
            continue
        active=False; prev=None; nxt=None; soc=float(bus['initial_soc_kwh'])
        for t in seq:
            ds=int(t['depart_min']); ae=int(t['arrive_min'])
            if ds <= cp < ae:
                active=True
                break
            if ae <= cp:
                prev=t; soc -= float(t['energy_kwh'])
            if cp < ds:
                nxt=t
                break
        if active or soc + 1e-8 < thr:

        arrive=0 if prev is None else int(prev['arrive_min']) + dead[(prev['end_location'],'DEPOT')]
        leave=math.inf if nxt is None else int(nxt['depart_min']) - dead[('DEPOT',nxt['start_location'])]
        if arrive <= cp <= leave:
            count += 1
    assert count >= need, (cp,count,need)
print('external_restoration_audit_ok')

result20. Command output
# Problem Statement
The depot must decide which visible trips to operate, which bus operates each trip, and when each bus charges at the depot. The schedule is revised online as new trips, charger faults, and grid limits are revealed.

The public data contains buses, chargers, bus-charger compatibility, fixed passenger trips, travel times between terminals, time-varying grid limits, charger outages, and reserve requirements. Trip times are fixed by the published service plan. Charging can only happen at the depot.

You are the control desk for a battery-electric bus depot running a full service day under online updates. At the current step, use only the trip, grid, charger, and event data visible in `/app`.

Hard operating rules:

- Every visible trip needs exactly one `trip` row.
- Critical trips must be operated. Non-critical trips may be canceled but incur a penalty.
- A bus may operate a trip only if its class and low-floor capability satisfy the trip.
- Each bus follows one chronological chain of trips and depot charging sessions. Include enough deadhead time between terminal locations and the depot.
- Battery state must never exceed the bus maximum or fall below its minimum. The bus must have at least the trip-specific start reserve before departure.
- A charger serves only one bus at a time, with its setup buffer between sessions. A bus can use only compatible chargers.
- Charging is forbidden during listed charger outages.
- Time-varying feeder and site caps limit simultaneous depot charging power.
- Event packets may add charger-session safety limits. These cap new charging starts and the number of fast chargers that can be active in a feeder or across the depot during the listed incident window.
- Event packets may add bus-level derating notices. A derated bus has a lower charging rate and may consume more energy on trips that overlap the notice.
- Reserve checkpoints require enough idle buses at the depot above the listed SOC threshold.
- Later replanning steps freeze near-term trip commitments and already-started charging sessions from the previous accepted plan.

The objective is to minimize total operating cost: service cancellation penalties, electricity purchases, carbon cost, battery-stress cost from high-power charging, and change penalties for modifying non-frozen previous commitments.

Data values are deliberately non-round because they represent telemetry and operating estimates rather than a cleaned classroom instance.

## Current Required Artifacts

For the initial planning step, write:

- `/app/submissions/model_initial.md`
- `/app/submissions/solve_initial.py`
- `/app/submissions/initial_plan.csv`
- `/app/submissions/solve_log_initial.md`

Every plan CSV must use this exact header:

```csv
row_type,item_id,bus_id,trip_id,charger_id,start_min,end_min,energy_kwh,decision
```

Use `trip` rows for every visible service trip. A trip row has `decision=operate` or `decision=cancel`; operated trips must name a bus, canceled trips leave `bus_id` blank. Use optional `charge` rows to describe depot charging sessions; each charge row needs a unique `item_id`, bus, charger, start minute, end minute, and delivered kWh.

result3. Command output
---
name: advanced-mip-patterns
description: Use when a task involves fixed charges, logical implications, min/max choices, sequencing, no-overlap, piecewise costs, routing-state logic, or other advanced MIP modeling patterns.
metadata:
  short-description: Advanced MIP formulation patterns
---

# Advanced MIP Patterns

## Use when

- The formulation needs logical implications, fixed charges, activation decisions, ordering, no-overlap, disjunctions, min/max, absolute values, tiers, route state, or resource calendars.
- A simple LP/MIP is producing unrealistic behavior because decisions are not linked tightly enough.
- The model is slow or infeasible and the likely cause is weak big-M logic, excessive pairwise variables, or missing state transitions.

## Do not use when

- A continuous LP is sufficient and adding binaries would not represent a real decision.
- The task is better handled by a specialized CP-SAT, constraint programming, nonlinear, or simulation method and the user did not require MIP.
- The user only needs a high-level explanation; do not introduce advanced constructs unless they clarify the actual model.

## General boundary rules

- Add binaries only for real yes/no choices, disjunctions, or nonconvex structure.
- Prefer the tightest formulation that matches the business rule; avoid decorative binaries.
- Use solver-native constructs when they improve clarity and are supported by the target environment.
- If a construct makes the model much slower, compare it with a simpler valid alternative before keeping it.

## Fixed charge and activation

- Use a binary `y` for whether an activity/resource is active.
- Link activity with a tight bound: `x <= U * y`.
- Add lower activation only when the business requires minimum use: `x >= L * y`.
- Charge fixed cost once through `fixed_cost * y`, not per unit.
- Do not force `y = 1` whenever `x = 0` unless the activity is mandatory.
- If there are startup/shutdown costs over time, separate active state from transition state.

## Logical rules

- Prefer solver-native indicator constraints for simple if-then rules when they keep the model clearer.
- If using big-M, derive M from data bounds and keep one M per rule family when possible.
- For either-or choices, use binaries whose sum is exactly one or at most one, matching the business rule.
- Distinguish implication (`A -> B`) from equivalence (`A <-> B`); the reverse direction often needs a separate constraint.
- For conditional capacity, link both activity and resource use to the condition.

## Min, max, absolute value

- For `z = max(a, b)`, add `z >= a`, `z >= b`, and objective pressure if minimizing z. Use binaries only when equality is needed without objective pressure.
- For absolute deviation `d = |x - target|`, add `d >= x - target` and `d >= target - x`.
- For service-level penalties, separate hard feasibility from soft penalty variables.
- For `min`, either transform with signs or add binaries when equality is not enforced by the objective.
- For ratio constraints, cross-multiply only when denominators are constant or safely bounded away from zero.

## Sequencing and no-overlap

- For jobs sharing a resource, use order binary `before[i,j]`.
- Typical pattern:
  - `start[j] >= end[i] - M * (1 - before[i,j])`
  - `start[i] >= end[j] - M * before[i,j]`
- Compute M from the scheduling horizon, not from an arbitrary large number.
- Include setup, cleanup, travel, rest, or turnover time in the correct side of the ordering constraint.
- Create pairwise order variables only for pairs that can share a resource or conflict.
- If time is discrete and the horizon is small, a time-indexed model may be cleaner than pairwise disjunctions.

## Piecewise and tiers

- Use solver-native piecewise-linear features for convex piecewise costs when suitable.
- For nonconvex tiers, use explicit segment variables and segment activation binaries.
- Ensure tier quantities sum to the original activity and each tier has its own upper bound.
- Distinguish incremental tiers from all-units tiers; their formulations and costs differ.
- Check whether discount tiers create nonconvex incentives that require binary selection.

## State and inventory logic

- State variables need initial conditions, transition equations, and terminal requirements if the business specifies them.
- Route or schedule state should be advanced in event order, not checked only row by row.
- Recovery, restock, cleaning, cooldown, maintenance, and rest rules often require cumulative state or last-event logic.
- If state can reset, model the reset trigger and its resource/time cost.

## Debugging advanced models

- First solve a relaxed or smaller instance and inspect whether the intended binaries activate.
- Add named constraints so IIS output points to business rules.
- If the reference solution is infeasible, replay the relevant state transitions manually before changing the evaluator.
- If the LP relaxation is very weak, inspect big-M values, missing lower/upper links, and symmetry.
- If the model is unexpectedly infeasible, test each advanced rule family on a tiny instance with a known feasible solution.

# OR PySCIPOpt

## Use when

- The task asks for a Python optimization model using SCIP or `pyscipopt`.
- Existing solver code imports `pyscipopt`, sets SCIP parameters, writes `.lp`/`.mps`, or calls a SCIP-based checker.
- The user asks to run, debug, speed up, validate, or explain a SCIP-based solve.

## Do not use when

- The task only needs a mathematical formulation and no solver-specific code.
- The project clearly uses a different solver stack, such as Pyomo with CBC/HiGHS, OR-Tools CP-SAT, PuLP, CPLEX, or cvxpy, unless the user asks to port it to PySCIPOpt.
- The model is nonlinear or stochastic in a way that SCIP cannot handle directly; first decide whether it can be linearized or approximated.

## Environment rule

Use the Python environment specified by the user or the project. In this ORClaw workspace, the default solver environment is the `or` conda environment with PySCIPOpt available:

```bash
conda run -n or python <script.py>
```

If `pyscipopt` is missing, report the missing dependency, identify the command that failed, and try the project-specified environment before changing solver stacks.

## Workflow

1. Read the task brief and input files before coding.
2. Write the full mathematical model first: sets, parameters, decision variables, objective, and constraints.
3. Implement the model with explicit tuple keys and named constraints.
4. Solve with the selected Python command.
5. Export the solution using the exact schema required by the task.
6. Validate with the task checker or an independent recomputation of feasibility and objective.
7. If the solution is not accepted, diagnose whether the issue is formulation, data parsing, export formatting, tolerance, or evaluator mismatch before changing the objective or constraints.

## PySCIPOpt patterns

- Use `from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum`.
- Create sparse variable dictionaries explicitly, for example:

```python
x = {(i, j, t): model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"x[{i},{j},{t}]") for i, j, t in keys}
```

- Use `quicksum(...)` for large linear expressions.
- Use `model.addCons(expr <= rhs, name="capacity[k,t]")` for constraints.
- Use `model.setObjective(expr, "minimize")` or `model.setObjective(expr, "maximize")`.
- Set reproducibility and limits with SCIP parameters:

```python
model.setParam("limits/gap", 0.0005)
model.setParam("limits/time", time_limit)
model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)
```

- After `model.optimize()`, inspect `model.getStatus()` and only read values if a solution exists.
- Read variable values with `model.getVal(x[key])`, not `x[key].X`.
- Read objective and bound with `model.getObjVal()` and `model.getDualbound()`.
- Read relative gap with `model.getGap()` when available.

## Modeling hygiene

- Calibrate every big-M from data. If a tight M is not obvious, derive a bound and document it in a short comment.
- Keep feasibility checks, objective recomputation, and reference-optimality comparison separate.
- Do not round exported decision values unless the schema explicitly requires it.
- For benchmark work, treat the evaluator or checker as the scoring authority and the reference solver as a validation baseline.
- Keep solver logs or a concise solve summary when the user needs auditability.
- Avoid changing data to make a model feasible unless the task is explicitly to design or repair an instance.

## Debugging boundaries

- If a model is infeasible, inspect hard lower bounds, conservation equations, and mutually exclusive binary logic before relaxing constraints.
- If objective values differ, recompute each objective component from exported decisions.
- If runtime is high, first remove dominated variables, tighten big-M values, add eligibility filters, and check whether binaries are really necessary.
- If SCIP returns an incumbent with a gap, say so clearly; do not call it exact optimality unless the status and gap support that claim.
- If an evaluator rejects a solution that the model considers feasible, replay the rejected rule directly from the exported file before changing solver code.

## Useful commands

```bash
python -c "import pyscipopt; print(pyscipopt.__version__)"
conda run -n or python -c "import pyscipopt; print(pyscipopt.__version__)"
<python-command> <solver-script.py>
<python-command> <checker-script.py> --solution <solution-file> --env-dir <task-dir>
```

# Optimization Modeling Core

## Use when

- The user gives a word problem, business process, paper scenario, or benchmark prompt and expects a formal optimization model.
- Solver code exists but the issue appears to be a missing or incorrect formulation.
- The task asks for a complete model, not only a heuristic, simulation, or data summary.

## Do not use when

- The task is purely about software plumbing, visualization, data cleaning, or report writing.
- The problem is already fully formulated and the user only asks for code translation into a specific solver; use the solver-specific skill as the primary guide.
- The request is exploratory and the user explicitly wants brainstorming without implementation.

## Model-first workflow

1. Restate the business decision in plain language.
2. Identify sets, indices, units, and time granularity.
3. Extract parameters from data files and confirm units.
4. Define decision variables with domains and business meaning.
5. Write the objective with every cost, reward, penalty, and service term.
6. Write constraints grouped by operational rule.
7. Check edge cases: missing eligibility, impossible demand, fixed commitments, and terminal-state requirements.
8. Only then implement code.

## Ambiguity handling

- If the brief is ambiguous but data and examples imply a convention, state the assumption and continue.
- If ambiguity changes feasibility, objective direction, or deliverable schema, ask before coding or implement the least risky interpretation with a clear note.
- If units conflict across files, normalize units explicitly before modeling.
- If a hard requirement conflicts with data, diagnose the conflict before adding slack variables.

## Formulation checklist

- Every row in an input table should either become a parameter, an eligibility rule, or be explicitly irrelevant.
- Every decision variable should appear in at least one binding constraint and usually in the objective.
- Demand balance should use the correct equality or inequality direction; do not weaken equality to `>=` unless surplus is genuinely allowed.
- Capacity constraints should include activation/open/setup binaries when the business says inactive resources cannot produce, ship, or serve.
- Shortage, backlog, overtime, outsourcing, and postponement variables need explicit cost and bounds.
- Multi-period models need state transition equations and initial/terminal states.
- If the user asks for explanation, describe the modeling logic in business terms after solving.

## Constraint boundary rules

- Hard constraints represent physical impossibility, policy mandates, legal requirements, or explicit benchmark feasibility rules.
- Soft constraints need penalty variables or objective terms; do not silently turn hard constraints into penalties.
- Service levels should specify numerator, denominator, eligible population, and time window.
- Fairness constraints should define the comparison group and whether unavailable resources count.
- Logical implications need both directions only when the business rule says equivalence, not merely implication.

## Objective boundary rules

- Confirm whether the problem minimizes cost, maximizes profit, maximizes service, or uses lexicographic priorities.
- Do not mix penalties with primary economics unless their scale is intentional.
- Include fixed costs only once per activated entity.
- Include revenue only for fulfilled demand or accepted jobs, not for unmet or canceled work.
- Keep reporting metrics separate from optimized objective terms unless the prompt explicitly optimizes them.

## Implementation expectations

- Use structured parsing for CSV/JSON instead of manual string manipulation.
- Keep solver code, solution export, and validation code clearly separated.
- For benchmark or task environments, read the provided brief before coding and follow the expected output schema exactly.
- When comparing two answers, diagnose concrete formulation differences and objective consequences, not only feasibility.
- Write exported solutions at the precision expected by the checker; avoid unnecessary rounding.
- Preserve the input schema and output schema unless the user asks to redesign the benchmark.

## Verification boundary

- Verify feasibility independently from solver status when an evaluator or checker exists.
- Recompute objective from exported artifacts, not only from in-memory solver expressions.
- For benchmark tasks, create at least one negative check for each newly added rule when practical.
- For paper or real-world tasks, separate what is directly supported by the source from synthetic or assumed data.

## Red flags

- Objective terms that are counted twice or charged at the wrong marginal rate.
- Binary variables not linked tightly enough to continuous activity.
- Time periods shifted by one index.
- Average or fairness constraints that ignore the denominator or eligibility set.
- Public prompt files that reveal solver-ready variables or formulas when the benchmark is meant to test modeling translation.
- A feasible solution that exploits missing eligibility, free disposal, negative cycles, or unbounded production.
- A model that reports optimality after a time limit, infeasible status, or missing incumbent.

# Problem Statement
The depot must decide which visible trips to operate, which bus operates each trip, and when each bus charges at the depot. The schedule is revised online as new trips, charger faults, and grid limits are revealed.

The public data contains buses, chargers, bus-charger compatibility, fixed passenger trips, travel times between terminals, time-varying grid limits, charger outages, and reserve requirements. Trip times are fixed by the published service plan. Charging can only happen at the depot.

You are the control desk for a battery-electric bus depot running a full service day under online updates. At the current step, use only the trip, grid, charger, and event data visible in `/app`.

Hard operating rules:

- Every visible trip needs exactly one `trip` row.
- Critical trips must be operated. Non-critical trips may be canceled but incur a penalty.
- A bus may operate a trip only if its class and low-floor capability satisfy the trip.
- Each bus follows one chronological chain of trips and depot charging sessions. Include enough deadhead time between terminal locations and the depot.
- Battery state must never exceed the bus maximum or fall below its minimum. The bus must have at least the trip-specific start reserve before departure.
- A charger serves only one bus at a time, with its setup buffer between sessions. A bus can use only compatible chargers.
- Charging is forbidden during listed charger outages.
- Time-varying feeder and site caps limit simultaneous depot charging power.
- Event packets may add charger-session safety limits. These cap new charging starts and the number of fast chargers that can be active in a feeder or across the depot during the listed incident window.
- Event packets may add bus-level derating notices. A derated bus has a lower charging rate and may consume more energy on trips that overlap the notice.
- Reserve checkpoints require enough idle buses at the depot above the listed SOC threshold.
- Later replanning steps freeze near-term trip commitments and already-started charging sessions from the previous accepted plan.

The objective is to minimize total operating cost: service cancellation penalties, electricity purchases, carbon cost, battery-stress cost from high-power charging, and change penalties for modifying non-frozen previous commitments.

Data values are deliberately non-round because they represent telemetry and operating estimates rather than a cleaned classroom instance.

## Current Required Artifacts

For the initial planning step, write:

- `/app/submissions/model_initial.md`
- `/app/submissions/solve_initial.py`
- `/app/submissions/initial_plan.csv`
- `/app/submissions/solve_log_initial.md`

Every plan CSV must use this exact header:

```csv
row_type,item_id,bus_id,trip_id,charger_id,start_min,end_min,energy_kwh,decision
```

Use `trip` rows for every visible service trip. A trip row has `decision=operate` or `decision=cancel`; operated trips must name a bus, canceled trips leave `bus_id` blank. Use optional `charge` rows to describe depot charging sessions; each charge row needs a unique `item_id`, bus, charger, start minute, end minute, and delivered kWh.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import json
import math
import os
import time
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum

ROOT = Path("/app")
DATA_DIR = ROOT / "data"
SUB_DIR = ROOT / "submissions"

PLAN_PATH = SUB_DIR / "initial_plan.csv"
LOG_PATH = SUB_DIR / "solve_log_initial.md"

EPS = 1e-8
SCIP_GAP = 0.0005
DEFAULT_TIME_LIMIT = 30.0
PLAN_HEADER = [
    "row_type",
    "item_id",
    "bus_id",
    "trip_id",
    "charger_id",
    "start_min",
    "end_min",
    "energy_kwh",
    "decision",
]


def read_csv(path: Path) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
    with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        return list(csv.DictReader(handle))


def to_int(value: str) -> int:
    return int(float(value))


def to_float(value: str) -> float:
    return float(value)


def fmt_number(value: float) -> str:
    if abs(value - round(value)) <= EPS:
        return str(int(round(value)))
    text = f"{value:.8f}".rstrip("0").rstrip(".")
    return text if text else "0"


def compatible(bus: Dict[str, str], trip: Dict[str, str]) -> bool:
    if int(bus["low_floor"]) < int(trip["low_floor_required"]):
        return False
    bus_class = bus["bus_class"]
    req = trip["bus_class_required"]
    if req == "standard":
        return bus_class in {"standard", "artic"}
    return bus_class == req


def overlap(start_a: float, end_a: float, start_b: float, end_b: float) -> float:
    return max(0.0, min(end_a, end_b) - max(start_a, start_b))


def load_data() -> Dict[str, object]:
    buses = {row["bus_id"]: row for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "buses.csv")}
    chargers = {row["charger_id"]: row for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "chargers.csv")}
    trips = [row for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "trips.csv")]
    current_step = json.loads((DATA_DIR / "current_step.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    visible_step = int(current_step["visible_step"])
    trips = [row for row in trips if int(row["reveal_step"]) <= visible_step]
    trips.sort(key=lambda row: (to_int(row["depart_min"]), row["trip_id"]))
    deadhead = {
        (row["from_location"], row["to_location"]): to_int(row["minutes"])
        for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "deadhead_times.csv")
    }
    compat = {(row["bus_id"], row["charger_id"]) for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "bus_charger_compatibility.csv")}
    grid_periods = [
        {
            "start": to_int(row["start_min"]),
            "end": to_int(row["end_min"]),
            "north_cap_kw": to_float(row["north_cap_kw"]),
            "south_cap_kw": to_float(row["south_cap_kw"]),
            "site_cap_kw": to_float(row["site_cap_kw"]),
            "price_per_kwh": to_float(row["price_per_kwh"]),
            "carbon_kg_per_kwh": to_float(row["carbon_kg_per_kwh"]),
        }
        for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "grid_periods.csv")
    ]
    outages = [
        row
        for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "charger_outages.csv")
        if row["charger_id"] != "NONE"
    ]
    session_limits = [
        row
        for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "charger_session_limits.csv")
        if row["limit_id"] != "BASE_NO_RESTRICTION"
    ]
    derates = [
        row
        for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "bus_event_derates.csv")
        if row["bus_id"] != "NONE"
    ]
    reserves = [
        {
            "checkpoint_min": to_int(row["checkpoint_min"]),
            "min_buses": to_int(row["min_buses"]),
            "min_soc_kwh": to_float(row["min_soc_kwh"]),
        }
        for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "reserve_requirements.csv")
    ]
    return {
        "buses": buses,
        "chargers": chargers,
        "trips": trips,
        "deadhead": deadhead,
        "compat": compat,
        "grid_periods": grid_periods,
        "outages": outages,
        "session_limits": session_limits,
        "derates": derates,
        "reserves": reserves,
        "current_step": current_step,
    }


def build_baseline_plan(data: Dict[str, object]) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
    rows: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
    for trip in data["trips"]:
        rows.append(
            {
                "row_type": "trip",
                "item_id": "",
                "bus_id": trip["block_hint"],
                "trip_id": trip["trip_id"],
                "charger_id": "",
                "start_min": "",
                "end_min": "",
                "energy_kwh": "",
                "decision": "operate",
            }
        )
    return rows


def load_or_seed_plan(data: Dict[str, object]) -> Tuple[List[Dict[str, str]], bool]:
    if PLAN_PATH.exists():
        return parse_plan(PLAN_PATH), False
    rows = build_baseline_plan(data)
    write_plan(PLAN_PATH, rows)
    return rows, True


def write_plan(path: Path, rows: List[Dict[str, str]]) -> None:
    with path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(handle, fieldnames=PLAN_HEADER)
        writer.writeheader()
        for row in rows:
            writer.writerow({key: row.get(key, "") for key in PLAN_HEADER})


def parse_plan(path: Path) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
    with path.open(newline="", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        reader = csv.DictReader(handle)
        if reader.fieldnames != PLAN_HEADER:
            raise ValueError(f"CSV header mismatch: {reader.fieldnames}")
        return list(reader)


def effective_trip_energy(bus_id: str, trip: Dict[str, str], derates: List[Dict[str, str]]) -> float:
    energy = to_float(trip["energy_kwh"])
    depart = to_int(trip["depart_min"])
    arrive = to_int(trip["arrive_min"])
    multiplier = 1.0
    for row in derates:
        if row["bus_id"] != bus_id:
            continue
        start = to_int(row["start_min"])
        end = to_int(row["end_min"])
        if overlap(depart, arrive, start, end) > 0:
            multiplier = max(multiplier, to_float(row["energy_multiplier"]))
    return energy * multiplier


def charge_capacity_kwh(
    bus: Dict[str, str],
    charger: Dict[str, str],
    start: float,
    end: float,
    derates: List[Dict[str, str]],
) -> float:
    if end <= start + EPS:
        return 0.0
    points = {start, end}
    for row in derates:
        if row["bus_id"] != bus["bus_id"]:
            continue
        ds = to_int(row["start_min"])
        de = to_int(row["end_min"])
        if overlap(start, end, ds, de) > 0:
            points.add(float(ds))
            points.add(float(de))
    ordered = sorted(points)
    total = 0.0
    for left, right in zip(ordered, ordered[1:]):
        if right <= left + EPS:
            continue
        rate = min(to_float(bus["max_charge_kw"]), to_float(charger["power_kw"]))
        for row in derates:
            if row["bus_id"] != bus["bus_id"]:
                continue
            ds = to_int(row["start_min"])
            de = to_int(row["end_min"])
            if overlap(left, right, ds, de) > 0:
                rate = min(rate, to_float(row["max_charge_kw"]))
        total += rate * (right - left) / 60.0
    return total


def locate_checkpoint_state(events: List[Dict[str, object]], checkpoint: int) -> Tuple[Optional[Dict[str, object]], Optional[Dict[str, object]], float]:
    prev_event = None
    next_event = None
    soc = None
    for event in events:
        if event["start"] <= checkpoint < event["end"]:
            return event, event, event["soc_before"]
        if event["end"] <= checkpoint + EPS:
            prev_event = event
            soc = event["soc_after"]
        if checkpoint <= event["start"] - EPS:
            next_event = event
            break
    if soc is None:
        soc = events[0]["initial_soc"] if events else 0.0
    return prev_event, next_event, soc


def build_bus_timelines(
    plan_rows: List[Dict[str, str]],
    data: Dict[str, object],
    issues: List[Dict[str, object]],
) -> Dict[str, List[Dict[str, object]]]:
    buses = data["buses"]
    chargers = data["chargers"]
    compat = data["compat"]
    deadhead = data["deadhead"]
    derates = data["derates"]
    trip_lookup = {trip["trip_id"]: trip for trip in data["trips"]}

    timelines: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, object]]] = defaultdict(list)
    charger_sessions: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, object]]] = defaultdict(list)
    charge_rows: List[Dict[str, object]] = []

result40. Command output
    trip_rows_seen = set()
    item_ids = set()

    def add_issue(group: str, residual: float, detail: str) -> None:
        issues.append({"group": group, "residual": max(0.0, residual), "detail": detail})

    for row in plan_rows:
        row_type = row["row_type"]
        if row_type not in {"trip", "charge"}:
            add_issue("schema", 1.0, f"invalid row_type {row_type}")
            continue

        if row_type == "trip":
            trip_id = row["trip_id"]
            trip_rows_seen.add(trip_id)
            if trip_id not in trip_lookup:
                add_issue("coverage", 1.0, f"unknown trip_id {trip_id}")
                continue
            trip = trip_lookup[trip_id]
            decision = row["decision"]
            if decision not in {"operate", "cancel"}:
                add_issue("schema", 1.0, f"invalid trip decision for {trip_id}")
            if row["item_id"]:
                add_issue("nullability", 1.0, f"trip row must leave item_id blank for {trip_id}")
            if row["charger_id"] or row["start_min"] or row["end_min"] or row["energy_kwh"]:
                add_issue("nullability", 1.0, f"trip row must leave charge fields blank for {trip_id}")
            if decision == "cancel":
                if trip["priority"] == "critical":
                    add_issue("service", 1.0, f"critical trip canceled: {trip_id}")
                if row["bus_id"]:
                    add_issue("nullability", 1.0, f"canceled trip must leave bus blank: {trip_id}")
            else:
                bus_id = row["bus_id"]
                if bus_id not in buses:
                    add_issue("assignment", 1.0, f"unknown bus {bus_id} on {trip_id}")
                    continue
                bus = buses[bus_id]
                if not compatible(bus, trip):
                    add_issue("compatibility", 1.0, f"incompatible bus {bus_id} on {trip_id}")
                timelines[bus_id].append(
                    {
                        "type": "trip",
                        "trip_id": trip_id,
                        "start": float(to_int(trip["depart_min"])),
                        "end": float(to_int(trip["arrive_min"])),
                        "start_loc": trip["start_location"],
                        "end_loc": trip["end_location"],
                        "energy": effective_trip_energy(bus_id, trip, derates),
                        "min_start_soc": to_float(trip["min_start_soc_kwh"]),
                    }
                )
        else:
            if not row["item_id"]:
                add_issue("nullability", 1.0, "charge row must provide a unique nonblank item_id")
            elif row["item_id"] in item_ids:
                add_issue("schema", 1.0, f"duplicate charge item_id {row['item_id']}")
            else:
                item_ids.add(row["item_id"])
            if row["trip_id"]:
                add_issue("nullability", 1.0, f"charge row must leave trip blank: {row['item_id']}")
            if row["decision"] != "charge":
                add_issue("schema", 1.0, f"charge row must use decision=charge: {row['item_id']}")
            bus_id = row["bus_id"]
            charger_id = row["charger_id"]
            if bus_id not in buses:
                add_issue("assignment", 1.0, f"unknown charge bus {bus_id}")
                continue
            if charger_id not in chargers:
                add_issue("assignment", 1.0, f"unknown charger {charger_id}")
                continue
            if not row["start_min"] or not row["end_min"] or not row["energy_kwh"]:
                add_issue("nullability", 1.0, f"charge row missing numeric fields: {row['item_id']}")
                continue
            start = to_float(row["start_min"])
            end = to_float(row["end_min"])
            energy = to_float(row["energy_kwh"])
            if end <= start + EPS:
                add_issue("charge", 1.0, f"nonpositive charge duration: {row['item_id']}")
            if energy < -EPS:
                add_issue("charge", 1.0, f"negative charge energy: {row['item_id']}")
            bus = buses[bus_id]
            charger = chargers[charger_id]
            if (bus_id, charger_id) not in compat:
                add_issue("compatibility", 1.0, f"incompatible charger {charger_id} for bus {bus_id}")
            cap = charge_capacity_kwh(bus, charger, start, end, data["derates"])
            if energy > cap + EPS:
                add_issue("charge", (energy - cap) / max(1.0, cap), f"charge energy exceeds capacity: {row['item_id']}")
            session = {
                "type": "charge",
                "item_id": row["item_id"],
                "start": start,
                "end": end,
                "start_loc": "DEPOT",
                "end_loc": "DEPOT",
                "energy": energy,
                "charger_id": charger_id,
            }
            timelines[bus_id].append(session)
            charger_sessions[charger_id].append(session)
            charge_rows.append({"bus_id": bus_id, "charger_id": charger_id, "start": start, "end": end, "energy": energy})

    visible_trip_ids = {trip["trip_id"] for trip in data["trips"]}
    missing = visible_trip_ids - trip_rows_seen
    extra = trip_rows_seen - visible_trip_ids
    if missing:
        add_issue("coverage", len(missing) / max(1, len(visible_trip_ids)), f"missing trip rows: {sorted(missing)}")
    if extra:
        add_issue("coverage", len(extra) / max(1, len(visible_trip_ids)), f"extra trip rows: {sorted(extra)}")

    for charger_id, sessions in charger_sessions.items():
        charger = chargers[charger_id]
        setup = to_float(charger["setup_min"])
        sessions.sort(key=lambda event: (event["start"], event["end"], event.get("item_id", "")))
        for prev, nxt in zip(sessions, sessions[1:]):
            gap = nxt["start"] - prev["end"]
            if gap + EPS < setup:
                add_issue(
                    "charger_conflict",
                    (setup - gap) / max(1.0, setup),
                    f"charger {charger_id} lacks setup gap between {prev.get('item_id', '?')} and {nxt.get('item_id', '?')}",
                )

    for outage in data["outages"]:
        charger_id = outage["charger_id"]
        start = to_int(outage["start_min"])
        end = to_int(outage["end_min"])
        for session in charger_sessions.get(charger_id, []):
            if overlap(session["start"], session["end"], start, end) > 0:
                add_issue("outage", 1.0, f"charge {session.get('item_id', '?')} overlaps outage on {charger_id}")

    for bus_id, events in timelines.items():
        bus = buses[bus_id]
        events.sort(key=lambda event: (event["start"], 0 if event["type"] == "trip" else 1, event["end"]))
        current_loc = "DEPOT"
        current_time = 0.0
        soc = to_float(bus["initial_soc_kwh"])
        for event in events:
            event["initial_soc"] = to_float(bus["initial_soc_kwh"])
            travel = data["deadhead"].get((current_loc, event["start_loc"]))
            if travel is None:
                add_issue("timing", 1.0, f"missing deadhead from {current_loc} to {event['start_loc']}")
                travel = 10**9
            if current_time + travel > event["start"] + EPS:
                add_issue(
                    "timing",
                    (current_time + travel - event["start"]) / max(1.0, event["start"] - current_time),
                    f"bus {bus_id} cannot reach {event['type']} starting at {event['start']}",
                )
            if event["type"] == "trip":
                if soc + EPS < event["min_start_soc"]:
                    add_issue(
                        "soc",
                        (event["min_start_soc"] - soc) / max(1.0, event["min_start_soc"]),
                        f"bus {bus_id} below trip start reserve on {event['trip_id']}",
                    )
                event["soc_before"] = soc
                soc -= event["energy"]
                event["soc_after"] = soc
                if soc + EPS < to_float(bus["min_soc_kwh"]):
                    add_issue(
                        "soc",
                        (to_float(bus["min_soc_kwh"]) - soc) / max(1.0, to_float(bus["min_soc_kwh"])),
                        f"bus {bus_id} below minimum SOC after {event['trip_id']}",
                    )
            else:
                event["soc_before"] = soc
                soc += event["energy"]
                event["soc_after"] = soc
                if soc > to_float(bus["max_soc_kwh"]) + EPS:
                    add_issue(
                        "soc",
                        (soc - to_float(bus["max_soc_kwh"])) / max(1.0, to_float(bus["max_soc_kwh"])),
                        f"bus {bus_id} exceeds max SOC after charge {event['item_id']}",
                    )
            current_loc = event["end_loc"]
            current_time = event["end"]

    active_points = set()
    for row in charge_rows:
        active_points.add(row["start"])
        active_points.add(row["end"])
    for period in data["grid_periods"]:
        active_points.add(float(period["start"]))
        active_points.add(float(period["end"]))
    checkpoints = sorted(active_points)
    for left, right in zip(checkpoints, checkpoints[1:]):
        if right <= left + EPS:
            continue
        mid = 0.5 * (left + right)
        active = [row for row in charge_rows if row["start"] <= mid < row["end"]]
        if not active:
            continue
        site = 0.0
        north = 0.0
        south = 0.0
        for row in active:
            power = row["energy"] * 60.0 / max(EPS, row["end"] - row["start"])
            site += power
            feeder = data["chargers"][row["charger_id"]]["feeder"]
            if feeder == "north":
                north += power
            else:
                south += power
        matching = [period for period in data["grid_periods"] if period["start"] <= mid < period["end"]]
        if matching:
            period = matching[0]
            if site > period["site_cap_kw"] + EPS:
                add_issue("grid", (site - period["site_cap_kw"]) / max(1.0, period["site_cap_kw"]), f"site cap exceeded near {mid}")
            if north > period["north_cap_kw"] + EPS:
                add_issue("grid", (north - period["north_cap_kw"]) / max(1.0, period["north_cap_kw"]), f"north feeder cap exceeded near {mid}")
            if south > period["south_cap_kw"] + EPS:
                add_issue("grid", (south - period["south_cap_kw"]) / max(1.0, period["south_cap_kw"]), f"south feeder cap exceeded near {mid}")

    for limit in data["session_limits"]:
        start = to_int(limit["start_min"])
        end = to_int(limit["end_min"])
        scope = limit["scope"]
        new_sessions = [
            row
            for row in charge_rows
            if start <= row["start"] < end
            and (scope == "all" or data["chargers"][row["charger_id"]]["feeder"] == scope)
        ]
        if len(new_sessions) > to_int(limit["max_new_sessions"]):
            add_issue("session_limit", 1.0, f"new session limit exceeded for scope {scope}")

        time_points = {float(start), float(end)}
        for row in charge_rows:
            if data["chargers"][row["charger_id"]]["charger_type"] != "fast":
                continue
            if overlap(row["start"], row["end"], start, end) > 0:
                time_points.add(row["start"])
                time_points.add(row["end"])
        ordered = sorted(time_points)
        for left, right in zip(ordered, ordered[1:]):
            if right <= left + EPS:
                continue
            mid = 0.5 * (left + right)
            active_fast = [
                row
                for row in charge_rows
                if row["start"] <= mid < row["end"]
                and data["chargers"][row["charger_id"]]["charger_type"] == "fast"
                and (scope == "all" or data["chargers"][row["charger_id"]]["feeder"] == scope)
            ]
            if len(active_fast) > to_int(limit["max_fast_active"]):
                add_issue("session_limit", 1.0, f"fast active limit exceeded for scope {scope}")
                break

    trip_lookup = {trip["trip_id"]: trip for trip in data["trips"]}
    for reserve in data["reserves"]:
        checkpoint = reserve["checkpoint_min"]
        need = reserve["min_buses"]
        threshold = reserve["min_soc_kwh"]
        count = 0
        for bus_id, bus in buses.items():
            events = timelines.get(bus_id, [])
            if not events:
                if to_float(bus["initial_soc_kwh"]) + EPS >= threshold:

/bin/bash -lc "sed -n '521,860p' /app/submissions/solve_initial.py"
result42. Command output
                    count += 1
                continue
            prev_event = None
            next_event = None
            active = False
            soc = to_float(bus["initial_soc_kwh"])
            for event in events:
                if event["start"] <= checkpoint < event["end"]:
                    active = True
                    break
                if event["end"] <= checkpoint + EPS:
                    prev_event = event
                    soc = event["soc_after"]
                if checkpoint <= event["start"] - EPS:
                    next_event = event
                    break
            if active or soc + EPS < threshold:
                continue
            arrive_depot = 0.0
            if prev_event is not None:
                arrive_depot = prev_event["end"] + deadhead[(prev_event["end_loc"], "DEPOT")]
            leave_depot = math.inf
            if next_event is not None:
                leave_depot = next_event["start"] - deadhead[("DEPOT", next_event["start_loc"])]
            if arrive_depot <= checkpoint + EPS and checkpoint + EPS <= leave_depot:
                count += 1
        if count < need:
            add_issue("reserve", (need - count) / max(1.0, need), f"reserve checkpoint {checkpoint} has {count} buses, needs {need}")

    return timelines


def audit_plan(path: Path, data: Dict[str, object]) -> Dict[str, object]:
    issues: List[Dict[str, object]] = []
    rows = parse_plan(path)
    timelines = build_bus_timelines(rows, data, issues)
    operated = sum(1 for row in rows if row["row_type"] == "trip" and row["decision"] == "operate")
    canceled = sum(1 for row in rows if row["row_type"] == "trip" and row["decision"] == "cancel")
    charge_rows = [row for row in rows if row["row_type"] == "charge"]
    coverage = sum(1 for row in rows if row["row_type"] == "trip") / max(1, len(data["trips"]))
    violated_groups = sorted({issue["group"] for issue in issues})
    theta = max((issue["residual"] for issue in issues), default=0.0)

    cancel_penalty = 0.0
    trip_lookup = {trip["trip_id"]: trip for trip in data["trips"]}
    for row in rows:
        if row["row_type"] == "trip" and row["decision"] == "cancel":
            cancel_penalty += to_float(trip_lookup[row["trip_id"]]["cancel_penalty"])

    electricity_cost = 0.0
    carbon_metric = 0.0
    for row in charge_rows:
        start = to_float(row["start_min"])
        end = to_float(row["end_min"])
        energy = to_float(row["energy_kwh"])
        duration = max(EPS, end - start)
        for period in data["grid_periods"]:
            ov = overlap(start, end, period["start"], period["end"])
            if ov <= 0:
                continue
            share = energy * ov / duration
            electricity_cost += share * period["price_per_kwh"]
            carbon_metric += share * period["carbon_kg_per_kwh"]

    return {
        "rows": rows,
        "timelines": timelines,
        "issues": issues,
        "theta": theta,
        "violated_groups": violated_groups,
        "coverage": coverage,
        "operated": operated,
        "canceled": canceled,
        "charge_rows": len(charge_rows),
        "cancel_penalty": cancel_penalty,
        "electricity_cost": electricity_cost,
        "carbon_metric": carbon_metric,
        "objective_zero_certificate": canceled == 0 and len(charge_rows) == 0 and theta == 0.0,
    }


def objective_summary(audit: Dict[str, object]) -> Dict[str, Optional[float]]:
    battery_stress_cost = 0.0
    change_penalty = 0.0
    carbon_cost = 0.0 if audit["charge_rows"] == 0 else None
    total_objective = None
    if audit["objective_zero_certificate"]:
        total_objective = 0.0
        carbon_cost = 0.0
    return {
        "cancel_penalty": audit["cancel_penalty"],
        "electricity_cost": audit["electricity_cost"],
        "carbon_cost": carbon_cost,
        "battery_stress_cost": battery_stress_cost,
        "change_penalty": change_penalty,
        "total_objective": total_objective,
    }


def build_repair_model(data: Dict[str, object], baseline_rows: List[Dict[str, str]], time_limit: float) -> Tuple[Model, Dict[Tuple[str, str], object], Dict[str, object]]:
    buses = data["buses"]
    trips = data["trips"]
    deadhead = data["deadhead"]

    model = Model("initial_zero_charge_repair")
    model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)
    model.setParam("limits/gap", SCIP_GAP)
    model.setParam("limits/time", max(0.0, time_limit))

    x: Dict[Tuple[str, str], object] = {}
    z: Dict[str, object] = {}
    trip_lookup = {trip["trip_id"]: trip for trip in trips}
    baseline_bus = {
        row["trip_id"]: row["bus_id"]
        for row in baseline_rows
        if row["row_type"] == "trip" and row["decision"] == "operate"
    }

    for trip in trips:
        t = trip["trip_id"]
        z[t] = model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"cancel[{t}]")
        for bus_id, bus in buses.items():
            if not compatible(bus, trip):
                continue
            if deadhead[("DEPOT", trip["start_location"])] > to_int(trip["depart_min"]):
                continue
            x[(bus_id, t)] = model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"x[{bus_id},{t}]")

    for trip in trips:
        t = trip["trip_id"]
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(x[(bus_id, t)] for bus_id in buses if (bus_id, t) in x) + z[t] == 1,
            name=f"cover[{t}]",
        )
        if trip["priority"] == "critical":
            model.addCons(z[t] == 0, name=f"critical[{t}]")

    sorted_trips = trips
    for bus_id, bus in buses.items():
        initial_soc = to_float(bus["initial_soc_kwh"])
        min_soc = to_float(bus["min_soc_kwh"])
        capacity = to_float(bus["battery_kwh"])
        for idx_j, trip_j in enumerate(sorted_trips):
            t_j = trip_j["trip_id"]
            if (bus_id, t_j) not in x:
                continue
            earlier_energy = quicksum(
                effective_trip_energy(bus_id, trip_i, data["derates"]) * x[(bus_id, trip_i["trip_id"])]
                for trip_i in sorted_trips[:idx_j]
                if (bus_id, trip_i["trip_id"]) in x
            )
            model.addCons(
                initial_soc - earlier_energy >= to_float(trip_j["min_start_soc_kwh"]) - capacity * (1 - x[(bus_id, t_j)]),
                name=f"start_soc[{bus_id},{t_j}]",
            )
        total_energy = quicksum(
            effective_trip_energy(bus_id, trip, data["derates"]) * x[(bus_id, trip["trip_id"])]
            for trip in sorted_trips
            if (bus_id, trip["trip_id"]) in x
        )
        model.addCons(initial_soc - total_energy >= min_soc, name=f"end_soc[{bus_id}]")

    for bus_id in buses:
        for i, trip_i in enumerate(sorted_trips):
            t_i = trip_i["trip_id"]
            if (bus_id, t_i) not in x:
                continue
            for trip_j in sorted_trips[i + 1 :]:
                t_j = trip_j["trip_id"]
                if (bus_id, t_j) not in x:
                    continue
                feasible = to_int(trip_i["arrive_min"]) + deadhead[(trip_i["end_location"], trip_j["start_location"])] <= to_int(trip_j["depart_min"])
                if not feasible:
                    model.addCons(x[(bus_id, t_i)] + x[(bus_id, t_j)] <= 1, name=f"conflict[{bus_id},{t_i},{t_j}]")

    model.setObjective(
        quicksum(to_float(trip_lookup[t]["cancel_penalty"]) * z[t] for t in z),
        "minimize",
    )

    warm = model.createSol()
    for (bus_id, trip_id), var in x.items():
        if baseline_bus.get(trip_id) == bus_id:
            model.setSolVal(warm, var, 1.0)
    for trip_id, var in z.items():
        model.setSolVal(warm, var, 0.0 if trip_id in baseline_bus else 1.0)
    model.addSol(warm)
    return model, x, z


def solve_repair_model(data: Dict[str, object], baseline_rows: List[Dict[str, str]], time_limit: float) -> Dict[str, object]:
    model, x, z = build_repair_model(data, baseline_rows, time_limit)
    model.optimize()

    status = str(model.getStatus())
    n_solutions = model.getNSols()
    if n_solutions <= 0:
        return {
            "status": status,
            "n_solutions": n_solutions,
            "used": True,
            "rows": baseline_rows,
            "objective": None,
            "best_bound": model.getDualbound() if model.getStatus() is not None else None,
            "gap": None,
        }

    sol = model.getBestSol()
    trip_lookup = {trip["trip_id"]: trip for trip in data["trips"]}
    rows: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
    for trip in data["trips"]:
        t = trip["trip_id"]
        chosen_bus = ""
        for bus_id in data["buses"]:
            if (bus_id, t) in x and model.getSolVal(sol, x[(bus_id, t)]) > 0.5:
                chosen_bus = bus_id
                break
        decision = "cancel" if model.getSolVal(sol, z[t]) > 0.5 else "operate"
        rows.append(
            {
                "row_type": "trip",
                "item_id": "",
                "bus_id": chosen_bus if decision == "operate" else "",
                "trip_id": t,
                "charger_id": "",
                "start_min": "",
                "end_min": "",
                "energy_kwh": "",
                "decision": decision,
            }
        )

    gap = None
    try:
        gap = model.getGap()
    except Exception:
        gap = None

    return {
        "status": status,
        "n_solutions": n_solutions,
        "used": True,
        "rows": rows,
        "objective": model.getObjVal(),
        "best_bound": model.getDualbound(),
        "gap": gap,
    }


def guarded_performance_phase(
    incumbent_rows: List[Dict[str, str]],
    incumbent_audit: Dict[str, object],
) -> Dict[str, object]:
    incumbent_obj = objective_summary(incumbent_audit)["total_objective"]
    if incumbent_audit["theta"] > 0:
        return {
            "attempted": False,
            "accepted": False,
            "candidate_rows": incumbent_rows,
            "candidate_audit": incumbent_audit,
            "incumbent_objective": incumbent_obj,
            "candidate_objective": incumbent_obj,
            "best_bound": None,
            "gap": None,
            "reason": "skipped because rollback incumbent is not feasible",
        }
    if incumbent_obj == 0.0:
        return {
            "attempted": False,
            "accepted": False,
            "candidate_rows": incumbent_rows,
            "candidate_audit": incumbent_audit,
            "incumbent_objective": 0.0,
            "candidate_objective": 0.0,
            "best_bound": 0.0,
            "gap": 0.0,
            "reason": "incumbent already attains the public nonnegative lower bound 0",
        }
    return {
        "attempted": False,
        "accepted": False,
        "candidate_rows": incumbent_rows,
        "candidate_audit": incumbent_audit,
        "incumbent_objective": incumbent_obj,
        "candidate_objective": incumbent_obj,
        "best_bound": None,
        "gap": None,
        "reason": "no exact improvement operator was needed for this packet",
    }


def negative_audit_check(data: Dict[str, object], rows: List[Dict[str, str]]) -> bool:
    corrupted = [dict(row) for row in rows]
    for row in corrupted:
        if row["row_type"] == "trip" and row["trip_id"] == "T025":
            row["bus_id"] = ""
            break
    tmp_path = SUB_DIR / "_tmp_negative_check.csv"
    write_plan(tmp_path, corrupted)
    audit = audit_plan(tmp_path, data)
    tmp_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    return audit["theta"] > 0 or bool(audit["violated_groups"])

# Initial Model: Visible Trips at Minute 0

`/app/AGENT_BRIEF.md` is not present in the workspace, so this model uses the public rules in `/app/PROBLEM_STATEMENT.md`, `/app/EVENT_NOTICE.md`, and the visible files under `/app/data/`.

## Visible Decision Scope

- Time horizon: the currently visible full day, with `current_min = 0`.
- Trips: every row in `data/trips.csv` with `reveal_step <= visible_step`.
- Buses: all rows in `data/buses.csv`.
- Chargers: all rows in `data/chargers.csv`.
- Current packet facts:
  - no active charger outage,
  - no active bus derating,
  - no event-specific charger session restriction,
  - no frozen prior commitments.

## Stage Strategy

The solver uses a hybrid two-stage method.

1. Build a constructive incumbent from the published `block_hint` values.
2. Audit the exported artifact exactly against the public hard rules.
3. If the incumbent is infeasible, invoke a SCIP restricted repair MIP with a warm start.
4. If the incumbent is feasible and has no cancellations and no charging rows, stop in performance mode because every public objective term is nonnegative and the resulting objective is exactly `0`.

For this initial packet the constructive incumbent is already feasible:

- each visible trip is assigned to its hinted bus,
- no charging sessions are needed,
- all trip start-SOC and terminal SOC checks pass,
- deadhead timing between consecutive hinted trips passes,
- four unused buses remain at the depot all day, so each reserve checkpoint is satisfied.

Because the plan has no canceled trips and no charging rows, every public objective component is zero:

- cancellation penalties: `0`,
- electricity purchases: `0`,
- carbon cost: `0`,
- battery-stress cost: `0`,
- change penalties: `0` at the initial step.

That gives a certificate of optimality without expanding the charging MIP.

## Constructive Incumbent

For each visible trip `t`, let `hint(t)` be its `block_hint` bus. The incumbent sets:

- `operate(t) = 1`,
- `assign(hint(t), t) = 1`,
- no `charge` rows.

The audit then verifies:

- exact trip-row coverage,
- trip rows leave `item_id` blank and charge rows use unique nonblank `item_id` values,
- bus-class and low-floor compatibility,
- chronological feasibility from the depot to the first trip and between consecutive trips using `deadhead_times.csv`,
- battery state propagation:
  - initial SOC from `buses.csv`,
  - trip energy from `trips.csv`,
  - start reserve `min_start_soc_kwh`,
  - lower bound `min_soc_kwh`,
  - upper bound `max_soc_kwh`,
- reserve checkpoints from `reserve_requirements.csv`,
- CSV nullability rules from the problem statement.

## Restricted Repair MIP

The fallback MIP is only used if the constructive incumbent fails.

### Decision variables

- `x[b,t] in {0,1}`: bus `b` operates trip `t`.
- `z[t] in {0,1}`: trip `t` is canceled.

### Objective

Minimize

`sum_t cancel_penalty[t] * z[t]`

This is sufficient for restoration because all charging-related cost terms are nonnegative and the fallback MIP is entered only to repair hard-rule violations in the zero-charge incumbent.

### Constraints

- Trip coverage:
  - `sum_b x[b,t] + z[t] = 1` for every visible trip `t`.
- Critical service:
  - `z[t] = 0` for every critical trip.
- Bus-trip compatibility:
  - `x[b,t] = 0` if bus class or low-floor capability is insufficient.
  - `artic` buses may cover `standard` trips; `standard` buses may not cover `artic` trips.
- Depot reachability for the first assigned trip:
  - disallow `x[b,t] = 1` if `deadhead(DEPOT, start(t)) > depart(t)`.
- Pairwise time-feasibility:
  - for any pair with `depart(i) < depart(j)` and
    `arrive(i) + deadhead(end(i), start(j)) > depart(j)`,
    enforce `x[b,i] + x[b,j] <= 1`.
- Start-SOC reserve:
  - if bus `b` operates trip `t`, then
    `initial_soc[b] - sum_{k: depart(k) < depart(t)} energy[k] * x[b,k] >= min_start_soc[t]`.
- End-of-horizon minimum SOC:
  - `initial_soc[b] - sum_t energy[t] * x[b,t] >= min_soc[b]`.

The repair MIP is intentionally restricted. It is a restoration operator, not the primary optimizer for this packet.

## Independent Audit and `theta`

The script computes a hard-constraint residual summary directly from the exported CSV, not from solver variables. The main state vector includes:

- artifact presence and schema status,
- incumbent count,
- maximum normalized hard-constraint residual `theta`,
- number of violated hard-rule groups,
- integrality residual,
- decision-universe coverage,
- objective / best bound / relative gap when SCIP is used,
- elapsed time.

`theta = 0` is required before entering performance mode.

        lines.append(f"- best bound: `{fmt_number(performance_result['best_bound'])}`")
    if performance_result["gap"] is not None:
        lines.append(f"- relative gap: `{fmt_number(performance_result['gap'])}`")
    lines.append(f"- candidate hard-audit passed: `{performance_result['candidate_audit']['theta'] == 0}`")
    lines.append(f"- published new artifact: `{performance_result['accepted']}`")
    lines.append(f"- decision: `{performance_result['reason']}`")
    lines.append("")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append(f"- corrupted-copy rejection check passed: `{negative_check_passed}`")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("## Notes")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("- The current visible packet has no active charger outage, bus derate, or event-specific charger-session restriction.")
    lines.append("- No charging is required to serve all currently visible trips while preserving minimum SOC and checkpoint reserves.")
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"


def main() -> None:
    start = time.time()
    data = load_data()
    rollback_rows, seeded_plan = load_or_seed_plan(data)
    rollback_audit = audit_plan(PLAN_PATH, data)
    baseline_rows = build_baseline_plan(data)
    repair_result = {"used": False, "status": None, "n_solutions": 0, "objective": None, "best_bound": None, "gap": None}

    time_limit = min(
        DEFAULT_TIME_LIMIT,
        float(os.environ.get("ORCLAW_SOLVE_TIME_LIMIT_SECONDS", DEFAULT_TIME_LIMIT)),
    )

    if rollback_audit["theta"] > 0:
        repair_result = solve_repair_model(data, baseline_rows, time_limit)
        candidate_path = SUB_DIR / "_candidate_initial_plan.csv"
        write_plan(candidate_path, repair_result["rows"])
        candidate_audit = audit_plan(candidate_path, data)
        if candidate_audit["theta"] == 0:
            write_plan(PLAN_PATH, repair_result["rows"])
            rollback_rows = repair_result["rows"]
            rollback_audit = audit_plan(PLAN_PATH, data)
        candidate_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)

    performance_result = guarded_performance_phase(rollback_rows, rollback_audit)
    final_audit = audit_plan(PLAN_PATH, data)
    negative_check_passed = negative_audit_check(data, parse_plan(PLAN_PATH))
    elapsed = time.time() - start
    from pyscipopt import __version__ as pyscipopt_version

    LOG_PATH.write_text(
        build_log(
            data,
            final_audit,
            elapsed,
            pyscipopt_version,
            repair_result,
            negative_check_passed,
            seeded_plan,
            rollback_audit,
            performance_result,
        ),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    if final_audit["theta"] > 0:
        groups = ", ".join(final_audit["violated_groups"])
        raise SystemExit(f"Final artifact is infeasible; theta={final_audit['theta']}, groups={groups}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
